Implementation details for Adam on z:

```
def _optimize_z(p0, m, time_limit, rng, base_tau_factor=0.05, final_tau_factor=0.002):
    z = _logit(p0)
    state m,v...
    best_p=p0; best_score=score_fft(p)
    no_improve=0; it=0
    start...
    while time<limit:
       p=softmax(z)
       b=conv_square_fft(p)
       sc=2*m*max(b)
       if sc < best_score: update; no_improve=0
       else no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > restart_threshold: break/restart
       mval=max(b)
       tau = ... * mval
       w = soft_weights(b, tau)
       h = corr(p,w)
       mean = dot(p,h)
       g = p*(h - mean)
       # maybe add symmetrizing? no
       z=adam(z,g,lr,cap)
       z -= mean(z)
       if it%polish_interval: local random perturb best and evaluate; if improve reset z...
```

But Adam with infinite small gradient may keep no_improvement many. Use no_improve threshold 500-2000.     q = p - step*h
    # project onto simplex (sqrt can just clip+normalize? Not exact but okay)

```

 if a.size==m: return normalize(a)

def _adam_step(z,g, state, lr, cap):
  t=state['t']+1
  beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999,eps=1e-8
  state['m']=b1*state['m']+(1-b1)*g
  state['v']=b2*state['v']+(1-b2)*g*g
  mb=1-b1**t? Use running correction as parent.
  step=lr*mb/(sqrt(vb)+eps)
  z=z-step clipped
```

  # If time small, return best
  # Adam loop

  state = zeros
  lr=0.4; cap=0.8
  no=0; it=0; last_restart=0
  trick: use no_improve_count; if > 1000, reset z to logit(best)+noise, reset state.
  polish_interval = 2000 if m large else 500
  tau_factor = 0.05 initially, annealed with frac = time progress? But time may include restarts. Use iteration count.
  while time < end:
     p=softmax(z)
     b=conv(p)
     sc=2m*max(b) # p sum1
     if sc < best_score: best...
       # if improvement, maybe reduce cap? no
     no...
     # compute tau
     mval=max(b)
     frac=min(1,it/30000?) or time fraction
     tau=(0.05 -0.048*frac)*mval + 1e-12
     w=softmax(w)
     h=corr(p,w)
     mean=dot(p,h)
     g=p*(h-mean)
     # norm guard
     if not finite g: g=0
     z=adam(z,g,lrt, cap)
     z-=z.mean()
     # optional sym improvement every 1000
     if it%1000==0:
        ps=(p+p[::-1])/2; sc_s=score; if <best reset z
        # polish
     if no_improve > threshold:
        # restart from best or best_p with reverse/noise or alternative init
        noise=0.1*rng.normal; z=logit(best_p)+noise
        reset state; lr=max(0.1, lr*0.6); no=0; threshold*=1.2

Soft weights:
```
def _soft_weights(b, tau):
  m=b.max()
  if m<=0: return np.full_like(b, 1/(2m-1))
  z=(b-m)/tau
  # z<=0; clip min -700
  e=np.exp(np.clip(z, -700, 0.0))
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0: one at argmax
  return e/s
```

def _lbfgs_refine(z, m, meta, time_limit, tau_rel=0.002, scale=1e6):
  # measure initial cost
  out=objective(z)
  bmax=...; tau = max(1e-12, tau_rel*bmax)
  def fun(z):
     p=softmax(z); b=conv; bmax=...
     soft = tau*(bmax + log(sumexp((b-bmax)/tau)))? Equivalent to tau*logsumexp. Actually soft = tau*(bmax + log(sumexp((b-bmax)/tau))) = tau*logsumexp(b/tau). Good.
     val = 2*m*soft*scale
     if soft not finite: return big, zeros
     w=exp((b-bmax)/tau); normalize? same as softmax for weights for logsumexp. Since w normalized.
     h=corr(p,w); mean=dot(p,h); g=4*m*p*(h-mean)*scale
     return val,g
  res = minimize(fun, z, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': max_iter, 'maxls':10, 'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'disp':False})
  return res.x if better
```

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float)
  a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
  a=np.maximum(a,0)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
  return a/s
```
If a size 0 return ones(1).

Upsample:
```
def _upsample(a,m):
  a=_normalize(a)
  if a.size==m: return a.copy()
  if a.size==0: return ones/m
  x1=np.linspace(0,1,m)
  out=np.interp(x1, np.linspace(0,1,a.size), a)
  return _normalize(out)
```
Could use if `out` has zeros due interp? parent positive likely. Add small floor before normalize? `out=np.maximum(out,1e-12)`.

Logit/softmax:
```
def _logit(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float)
  a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
  z=np.log(a)
  z-=z.mean()
  return np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0)
```
But if some p underflow 0, log 0 -> floor. Good.

Softmax as above.

FFT meta:
```
def _meta(m):
  if m in cache: return cache[m]
  Lb=1 << max(0, (2*m-1)-1).bit_length() # for x>0
  Lc=1 << max(0, (3*m-2)-1).bit_length()
  idx=2*m-2 - np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
  cache[m] = (Lb,Lc,idx,2*m-1)
  return ...
```
For m=1, 2m-1=1, Lb=1? `(0).bit_length()`? Use function:
```
def _next_p2(x):
  x=int(max(1,x))
  return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

Conv square:
```
def _conv_square(p,L):
  A=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
  b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
  b = b[:2*len(p)-1]
  b = np.where(b>0, b, 0.0)
  return b
```
Maybe `A*A` complex; irfft. We can use `np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)`, A length L//2+1. Good.

`_score_fft(p)`:
```
m=len(p); s=p.sum(); if s<... inf
b=_conv_square(p,L)
mx=b.max()
return 2*m*mx/(s*s)
```
For normalized p, s=1.

Score + b for gradient: To avoid duplicate conv, in loop compute b once:
```
b=_conv_square(p,Lb); mx=unique max; sc=...
```

Correlate:
```
def _corr_p_w(p,w,Lc,idx):
  P=np.fft.rfft(p,Lc)
  Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],Lc)
  conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Wr,Lc)
  return conv[idx]
```
Check convolution formula: `np.fft.irfft(P*Wr)` gives linear convolution p * w_rev padded length Lc. If Lc >= len(p)+len(w)-1 =3m-2. `conv[idx]` with idx=2m-2 - arange(m). Good.

Soft weights:
```
def _weights(b, tau):
  m=max(b)
  if m<=0: return full 1/size
  z=(b-m)/tau
  e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,0))
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0 or not finite: w=zeros; w[argmax(b)]=1
  else w=e/s
  return w
```

Objective for LBFGS:
```
def _lbfgs_objective(z, m, Lb,Lc,idx, tau, scale=1e6):
   p=softmax(z); b=conv_square; mx=b.max(); if mx<=0 return 1e100 zeros
   zshift=(b-mx)/tau
   e=np.exp(np.clip(zshift,-700,0)); s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: s=1; zshift=zeros?; w argmax
   soft = tau*(mx + math.log(s))
   val = 2*m*soft*scale
   w=e/s
   h=corr(p,w); mean=dot(p,h); g=4*m*p*(h-mean)*scale
   if not finite: return val?, zeros
   return val,g
```
Need `math.log` import.
If s finite. Since zshift max 0, exp(0)=1, s>=1. Good.

Local polish implementation for p (using FFT score):
```
def _polish_p(p, rng, trials, meta):
  best=p.copy(); best_sc=score(p)
  for _ in range(trials):
    cand=p.copy()
    # random log noise low frequency
    # q = rng.normal(size=p.size); smooth by setting FFT of q to zero high freq
    q=rng.normal(size=p.size)
    Q=np.fft.rfft(q)
    keep=max(4, p.size//50)
    Q[keep:]=0
    q=np.fft.irfft(Q, n=p.size)*0.05
    cand *= np.exp(q)
    # random roll maybe
    if p.size>16 and rng.random()<0.3:
       off=int(rng.integers(-p.size//20,p.size//20+1)); cand=0.5*(cand+np.roll(cand,off)); 
    cand=_normalize(cand)
    sc=_score_fft(cand)
    if sc<best_sc: best=cand
  return best,best_sc
```
Polish every maybe 3000 iterations; trials=1-3. Cost: each trial FFT score + FFT for smoothing (rfft/irfft n) maybe 4x, okay if few. For n=8192, 3 trials ~10 FFTs =0.03s? fine.

But random low-frequency smoothing uses FFT of size n each trial; okay. Maybe use DCT basis instead.

Sym check every maybe 1000: `ps=(p+p[::-1])/2` score. Cost one score if compute only when check. Could compute from same b? Not easily. Use `_score_fft`. Check infrequent, e.g., every 5000. Since reversal score same, sym maybe. Fine.

_next_p2:
```
def _next_p2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(z):
  z=np.asarray(z,float)
  z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
  z=z-z.max()
  e=np.exp(z)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0: return ones/size
  return e/s
```
If z.max large? z centered, max maybe around tail. okay.

Logit:
```
def _logit(a):
 a=np.asarray(a,float)
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
 a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
 z=np.log(a)
 z-=z.mean()
 return np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0)
```
If a has all zeros? _normalize should not. For floor, size.

Weights: For tau <=0? set tau small.

Corr: Need `w` length `2*m-1`. In `_corr_p_w` if `w.size < 2*m-1`? We always pass. Use `wr = w[::-1]` view. `conv = np.fft.irfft(P * Wr, n=Lc)`. For `P*Wr`, `Wr` length Lc//2+1. Good. `conv` real length Lc. Index array int64. `return conv[idx]`.

Score: If p size m, `conv_square` uses Lb from meta. `b = np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=Lb)[:2*m-1]`. If m=1, b length1; A length? rfft(p,1) length1? 1D rfft of length1 returns length1? Yes. A*A length1; irfft n=1 returns length1.

Adam:
```
def _adam(z,g,state,lr,cap):
 dim=z.size
 if not finite g: g=zeros
 state['t'] +=1; b1=0.9,b2=0.999,eps=1e-8
 state['b1'] += 1-b1
 state['b2'] += 1-b2
 state['m'] = b1*state['m']+(1-b1)*g
 state['v'] = b2*state['v']+(1-b2)*(g*g)
 mhat=state['m']/state['b1']; vhat=state['v']/state['b2']
 step=lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
 if not finite step: zeros
 if cap not None: step=np.clip(step,-cap,cap)
 z=z-step

def _make_inits(m, rng, parent=None, best=None):
    inits=[]
    def add(x):
       try:
          a=np.asarray(x,float)
          if a.ndim!=1 or a.size!=m: return
          a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
          a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
          if a.sum()<=1e-300:
             a=np.ones(m)/m
          inits.append(a/a.sum())
       except: pass
    if parent is not None:
       up=_upsample(parent,m)
       add(up); add(up[::-1]); add((up+up[::-1])*0.5)
       # slightly noisy parent
       add(up*np.exp(0.03*rng.normal(m)))
       add(up*np.exp(0.08*rng.normal(m)))
    if best is not None and best.size != m:
       add(_upsample(best,m))
    add(np.ones(m)/m)
    if m > 2:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,m)
      for p in [0.3,0.7,1.0,1.5,2.0,-0.5,-1.0]:

`_lbfgs_refine`:
```
def _lbfgs_refine(z0,m,tau,time_limit,Lb,Lc,idx):
  if _sp_minimize is None or time_limit < 3: return z0
  def obj(z):
     p=...; b=...; mx=...
     if mx<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
     zshift=(b-mx)/tau
     e=np.exp(np.clip(zshift,-700,0)); s=e.sum()
     if s<=0: s=1; e=zeros; e[argmax]=1
     soft = mx + tau*math.log(s)
     val = 2*m*soft*1e6
     w=e/s
     h=...; mean=dot; g=4*m*p*(h-mean)*1e6
     if not finite g: zeros
     return val,g
  # measure cost
  t0=time.monotonic(); val,g=obj(z0.copy()); cost=max(1e-4,time.monotonic()-t0)
  maxit = int(min(400, max(20, time_limit/(max(1e-4,cost*8)))))
  # ensure enough remaining: if maxit*cost*8 > time_limit*0.8 set 20
  def fun(x): return obj(x)
  res = _sp_minimize(fun, z0, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxit,'maxls':10,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'disp':False})
  if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'): return np.asarray(res.x,float)
  return z0
```
But `ftol=1e-14` scaled value ~1e6, f differences maybe? okay. `gtol=1e-8`. If scaled, gradient norm maybe 0.1, gtol small.

`_lbfgs_polish`:
```
def _lbfgs_polish(p,m,time_limit,rng,Lb,Lc,idx):
  best=p; best_s=_score_fft(best)
  z=_logit(best)
  start_p=time.monotonic()
  for rel in (0.005,0.001,0.0003):
     remain = start_p + time_limit - time.monotonic()
     if remain < 20: break
     mxs=best_s/(2*m) if best_s>0 else 1e-9
     tau=max(1e-12, rel*mxs)
     tlim = min(remain-10, max(10, time_limit/len*1.2?)) # allocate maybe half first
     if rel==0.005: tlim = min(remain-10, 0.5*time_limit)
     else: tlim = remain-10
     z = _lbfgs_refine(z,m,tau,tlim,Lb,Lc,idx)
     p2=softmax(z); sc=_score_fft(p2)
     if finite and sc < best_s: best,p best_s
  return _normalize(best)
```
Need ensure tlim positive. Simpler: run first with `min(remain-10, max(10,0.4*remain))`, second with `min(remain-5, max(10,0.5*remain))`.

     Q=np.fft.rfft(q); keep=max(2,m//32); Q[keep:]=0; q=0.08*np.fft.irfft(Q,n=m)

Let's verify `_corr_p_w` formula with small example to ensure gradient direction not reversed.
Let p length 3 [p0,p1,p2], w=[w0,w1,w2,w3,w4]. h0 = ∑_d p_d w_{0+d}=p0w0+p1w1+p2w2. Formula conv(p, w_rev) with w_rev=[w4,w3,w2,w1,w0]. conv indices 0..6:
conv0=p0w4
conv1=p0w3+p1w4
conv2=p0w2+p1w3+p2w4

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
except Exception:
    _sp_minimize = None

TARGET = 1.50274349
_fft_cache = {}


def _next_p2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _meta(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m in _fft_cache:
        return _fft_cache[m]
    Lb = _next_p2(2 * m - 1)
    Lc = _next_p2(3 * m - 2)
    idx = 2 * m - 2 - np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    _fft_cache[m] = (Lb, Lc, idx, 2 * m - 1)
    return _fft_cache[m]


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _upsample(a, m):
    m = int(m)
    a = _normalize(a)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == m:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-12)
    return _normalize(out)


def _logit(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    z = np.log(a)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    return z


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _conv_square(p, Lb):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, n=Lb)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=Lb)
    b = b[:2 * p.size - 1]
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score_fft(p, Lb=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(p.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.inf
    if Lb is None:
        Lb, _, _, _ = _meta(m)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    b = _conv_square(p, Lb)
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * m * mx / (s * s))


def _corr_p_w(p, w, Lc, idx):
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, n=Lc)
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], n=Lc)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(P * Wr, n=Lc)
    return conv[idx]


def _soft_weights(b, tau):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if tau <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.full(b.size, 1.0 / float(b.size), dtype=np.float64)
    z = (b - mx) / tau
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -700.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def _adam_state(dim):
    return {
        "m": np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64),
        "v": np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64),
        "b1": 0.0,
        "b2": 0.0,
        "t": 0,
    }


def _adam(z, g, state, lr, cap):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros_like(g)
    b1, b2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
    state["t"] += 1
    state["b1"] += 1.0 - b1
    state["b2"] += 1.0 - b2
    state["m"] = b1 * state["m"] + (1.0 - b1) * g
    state["v"] = b2 * state["v"] + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
    mhat = state["m"] / state["b1"]
    vhat = state["v"] / state["b2"]
    step = float(lr) * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)):
        step = np.zeros_like(step)
    if cap is not None:
        step = np.clip(step, -float(cap), float(cap))
    z = z - step
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    return z


def _make_inits(m, rng, parent=None, best=None):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return [np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)]

    inits = []

    def add(x):
        try:
            a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if a.size != m:
                return
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            if float(a.sum()) <= 1e-300:
                a = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
            inits.append(a / float(a.sum()))
        except Exception:
            return

    if parent is not None:
        up = _upsample(parent, m)
        add(up)
        add(up[::-1])
        add(0.5 * (up + up[::-1]))
        add(np.maximum(up * np.exp(0.03 * rng.normal(size=m)), 1e-6))
        add(np.maximum(up * np.exp(0.08 * rng.normal(size=m)), 1e-6))

    if best is not None and best.size != m:
        add(_upsample(best, m))

    add(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m))

    if m > 2:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)

        for a in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            add((x + 1e-3) ** a)
            add(((1.0 - x) + 1e-3) ** a)

        for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            add(x ** a + (1.0 - x) ** a + 1e-3)

        add(x * (1.0 - x) + 1e-3)
        add(np.exp(-20.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2) + 1e-3)
        add(np.exp(-10.0 * (x - 0.30) ** 2) + 1e-3)
        add(np.exp(-10.0 * (x - 0.70) ** 2) + 1e-3)
        add(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2 + 1e-3)
        add(np.cos(np.pi * x) ** 2 + 1e-3)
        add(1.0 / (1.0 + 20.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2) + 1e-3)

        for scale in (0.25, 0.6):
            z = rng.normal(size=m) * scale
            Q = np.fft.rfft(z)
            keep = max(2, m // 32)
            if Q.size > keep:
                Q[keep:] = 0.0
            z = 0.25 * np.fft.irfft(Q, n=m)
            add(np.exp(z) + 1e-6)

    if len(inits) > 26:
        inits = inits[:26]

    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m))
    return inits


def _polish_p(p, rng, trials, Lb=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(p.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return p, np.inf
    if Lb is None:
        Lb, _, _, _ = _meta(m)
    best = p.copy()
    best_s = _score_fft(best, Lb)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        best_s = _score_fft(best, Lb)

    trials = int(max(0, trials))
    for _ in range(trials):
        cand = p.copy()

        if m > 4 and rng.random() < 0.85:
            q = rng.normal(size=m)
            Q = np.fft.rfft(q)
            keep = max(2, m // 25)
            if Q.size > keep:
                Q[keep:] = 0.0
            q = 0.08 * np.fft.irfft(Q, n=m)
            cand *= np.exp(q)

        if m > 8 and rng.random() < 0.35:
            span = max(1, m // 20)
            off = int(rng.integers(-span, span + 1))
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + np.roll(cand, off))

        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score_fft(cand, Lb)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_s = sc

    return best, best_s


def _lbfgs_refine(z0, m, tau, time_limit, Lb, Lc, idx):
    if _sp_minimize is None:
        return np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    z0 = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64) - float(np.mean(np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64)))
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return z0

    def obj(z):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        p = _softmax(z)
        b = _conv_square(p, Lb)
        mx = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros_like(z)

        zz = (b - mx) / tau
        zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, -700.0)
        zz = np.clip(zz, -700.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(zz)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(e)
            w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
            log_s = 0.0
        else:
            w = e / s
            log_s = math.log(s)

        soft = mx + tau * log_s
        val = 2.0 * float(m) * soft * 1.0e6

        h = _corr_p_w(p, w, Lc, idx)
        mean_h = float(np.dot(p, h))
        g = 4.0 * float(m) * p * (h - mean_h) * 1.0e6
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        return float(val), g

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _, g0 = obj(z0.copy())
    except Exception:
        return z0
    cost = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t0)
    if not np.isfinite(g0):
        return z0

    # Conservative cap so SciPy line search cannot consume the whole budget.
    maxit = int(min(220, max(12, int(time_limit / max(1e-4, cost * 18.0)))))
    maxit = max(8, maxit)

    def fun(x):
        return obj(x)

    try:
        res = _sp_minimize(
            fun,
            z0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": maxit,
                "maxls": 8,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if out.size == z0.size and np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
                return out
    except Exception:
        pass
    return z0


def _lbfgs_polish(p, m, time_limit, rng, Lb, Lc, idx):
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 15.0:
        return _normalize(p)

    p = _normalize(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_s = _score_fft(best, Lb)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        best_s = _score_fft(best, Lb)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        return best

    z = np.asarray(_logit(best), dtype=np.float64)
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    end_t = start_t + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    bmax = max(1e-300, best_s / (2.0 * float(m)))

    rels = (0.005, 0.001, 0.0003)
    for i, rel in enumerate(rels):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remain = end_t - now
        if remain <= 12.0:
            break
        alloc = remain * (0.55 if i == 0 else 0.45 if i == 1 else 1.0)
        alloc = max(8.0, min(alloc, remain - 8.0))
        tau = max(1e-12, rel * bmax / (1.0 if i == 0 else (2.0 if i == 1 else 4.0)))
        z = _lbfgs_refine(z, m, tau, alloc, Lb, Lc, idx)
        q = _softmax(z)
        sc = _score_fft(q, Lb)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
            best = q.copy()
            best_s = sc
            bmax = max(1e-300, best_s / (2.0 * float(m)))

    return _normalize(best)


def _optimize_m(m, inits, start_time, end_time, rng):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    Lb, Lc, idx, _ = _meta(m)

    best_p = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    best_score = _score_fft(best_p, Lb)

    for a in inits:
        a = _normalize(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
        if a.size != m:
            continue
        sc = _score_fft(a, Lb)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = a.copy()

    if best_score > np.inf / 2 or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_p = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        best_score = _score_fft(best_p, Lb)

    start_time = float(start_time)
    end_time = float(end_time)
    if end_time <= time.monotonic() + 2.0:
        return best_p

    z = np.asarray(_logit(best_p), dtype=np.float64)
    state = _adam_state(m)
    lr = 0.45
    base_cap = 0.7
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    restarts = 0
    restart_limit = 900 if m <= 1024 else 3200

    span = max(1e-3, end_time - start_time)
    lbfgs_reserve = 0.0
    if _sp_minimize is not None and m >= 512 and span > 120.0:
        lbfgs_reserve = min(180.0, max(30.0, 0.12 * span))
    adam_end = min(end_time, end_time - lbfgs_reserve)

    while time.monotonic() < adam_end:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= adam_end:
            break

        p = _softmax(z)
        b = _conv_square(p, Lb)
        mx = float(np.max(b))
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            p = _normalize(best_p)
            z = _logit(p) + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=m)
            state = _adam_state(m)
            no_improve += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        sc = float(2.0 * m * mx / (s * s))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            lr = min(0.85, lr * 1.01)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - start_time) / span))
        tau = max(1e-12, (0.08 - 0.072 * frac) * mx)
        w = _soft_weights(b, tau)

        # Near the end, also try a genuinely active-set subgradient, using
        # all coefficients within a small relative tolerance of the maximum.
        if frac > 0.75:
            delta = max(1e-12, 0.002 * mx)
            am = b >= (mx - delta)
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(am))
            if 2 <= cnt < max(120, m // 8):
                w = np.zeros_like(w)
                w[am] = 1.0 / float(cnt)

        h = _corr_p_w(p, w, Lc, idx)
        mean_h = float(np.dot(p, h))
        g = 4.0 * float(m) * p * (h - mean_h)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.zeros_like(p)

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        cap = base_cap
        if no_improve > restart_limit // 3:
            cap = min(cap, 0.25)
        if no_improve > restart_limit // 2:
            cap = min(cap, 0.08)

        if gnorm > 1e-14:
            z = _adam(z, g, state, lr, cap)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            nzmax = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
            if nzmax > 45.0:
                z = 45.0 * np.tanh(z / 45.0)

        if it % 2000 == 0 and now < adam_end - 5.0:
            ps = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
            scs = _score_fft(ps, Lb)
            if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = scs
                best_p = ps.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                z = _logit(best_p) + 0.02 * rng.normal(size=m)
                state = _adam_state(m)
                lr = 0.45

        if it % 5000 == 0 and now < adam_end - 25.0:
            pol, scpol = _polish_p(best_p, rng, trials=1, Lb=Lb)
            if np.isfinite(scpol) and scpol < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = scpol
                best_p = pol.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                z = _logit(best_p) + 0.02 * rng.normal(size=m)
                state = _adam_state(m)
                lr = 0.45

        if no_improve > restart_limit:
            if best_score < np.inf and len(inits) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.70:
                z = _logit(best_p) + 0.06 * rng.normal(size=m)
            else:
                ci = int(rng.integers(0, len(inits)))
                z = _logit(inits[ci]) + 0.10 * rng.normal(size=m)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            state = _adam_state(m)
            lr = max(0.15, lr * 0.72)
            no_improve = 0
            restarts += 1
            restart_limit = max(900, int(restart_limit * 1.22))
            if restarts > 14:
                break

        it += 1

    if _sp_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < end_time - 8.0:
        remain = max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic() - 8.0)
        if remain > 20.0:
            best_p = _lbfgs_polish(
                best_p,
                m,
                remain,
                rng,
                Lb,
                Lc,
                idx,
            )
            sc = _score_fft(best_p, Lb)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc

    return _normalize(best_p)


def _base_stage_time(L):
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 128:
        return 6.0
    if L <= 256:
        return 10.0
    if L <= 512:
        return 15.0
    if L <= 1024:
        return 25.0
    if L <= 2048:
        return 45.0
    if L <= 4096:
        return 80.0
    if L <= 8192:
        return 110.0
    return 150.0


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    fallback = np.array([0.5, 0.1, 0.4], dtype=np.float64)

    parent = None
    try:
        p0 = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p0.ndim == 1 and p0.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p0)):
            parent = np.maximum(p0, 0.0)
            if float(parent.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = parent / float(parent.sum())
            else:
                parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        parent_len = int(parent.size)
        current_p = parent.copy()
    else:
        parent_len = 127
        current_p = np.ones(parent_len, dtype=np.float64) / float(parent_len)

    best_global = current_p
    best_global_score = _score_fft(best_global) if best_global.size > 0 else np.inf

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
        return [float(x) for x in best_global]

    T = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    if T > 1050.0:
        target_max = 16384
    elif T > 750.0:
        target_max = 8192
    elif T > 400.0:
        target_max = 4096
    elif T > 180.0:
        target_max = 2048
    else:
        target_max = 1024

    lengths = set()
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        lengths.add(parent.size)
    for L in (256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384):
        lengths.add(L)

    if parent is not None and parent.size <= target_max:
        lengths = {L for L in lengths if 1 <= L <= target_max}
    else:
        lengths = {max(1, target_max)}

    if not lengths:
        lengths = {max(1, target_max)}

    lengths = sorted(lengths)
    if not lengths or lengths[-1] < max(1, parent_len if parent is not None else 1):
        lengths.append(max(1, target_max))
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

    reserve = max(25.0, 0.05 * (deadline - time.monotonic()))
    final_min = max(30.0, min(160.0, 0.15 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
    pre_avail = max(5.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - reserve - final_min)

    pre_lengths = lengths[:-1]
    base = {L: _base_stage_time(L) for L in pre_lengths}
    base_sum = float(sum(base.values()))
    pre_scale = min(1.0, pre_avail / base_sum) if base_sum > 0 else 0.0
    stage_times = [max(3.0, base[L] * pre_scale) for L in pre_lengths]
    pre_used = float(sum(stage_times))

    try:
        idx_len = 0
        for L in lengths:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= deadline - reserve:
                break

            if L < max(lengths[-1], 1) or idx_len < len(lengths) - 1:
                time_limit = stage_times[idx_len]
                idx_len += 1
            else:
                time_limit = max(1.0, min(delay_left := deadline - now - reserve,
                                           max(30.0, (deadline - now) - reserve)))

            time_limit = max(1.0, min(time_limit, max(0.0, deadline - now - 2.0)))
            if time_limit <= 2.0:
                continue

            p_start = _upsample(current_p, L) if current_p.size != L else current_p
            inits = _make_inits(L, rng, parent=p_start, best=best_global)

            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - reserve:
                pL = p_start
            else:
                pL = _optimize_m(
                    L,
                    inits,
                    time.monotonic(),
                    min(time.monotonic() + time_limit, deadline - 2.0),
                    rng,
                )

            pL = _normalize(pL)
            sc = _score_fft(pL)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_global_score - 1e-12:
                    best_global_score = sc
                    best_global = pL.copy()
                current_p = pL.copy()
                if best_global.size > 0:
                    best_global = pL.copy()
                    best_global_score = sc
            else:
                current_p = pL

            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - reserve:
                break

    except Exception:
        pass

    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(arr):
        try:
            a = _normalize(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
            if a.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                candidates.append(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_candidate(current_p)
    add_candidate(best_global)
    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate(parent)
    if best_global.size > 1:
        add_candidate(0.5 * (best_global + best_global[::-1]))
    add_candidate(fallback)

    scored = []
    for arr in candidates:
        try:
            sc = _score_fft(arr)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, arr.copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if scored:
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    else:
        scored = [(np.inf, fallback.copy())]

    final_list = None
    final_exact = np.inf

    # Validate a few finite candidates with the authoritative evaluator.
    # Exact evaluator uses direct convolution, so keep this lightweight.
    for sc, arr in scored[:3]:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 10.0:
            break
        if arr.size > 4096 and (deadline - now) < 25.0:
            continue
        try:
            lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
            if not lst or any(not np.isfinite(x) for x in lst):
                continue
            s = float(np.sum(lst))
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            vs = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if np.isfinite(vs) and vs < final_exact:
                final_exact = vs
                final_list = lst
        except Exception:
            continue

    if final_list is None:
        # Last resort: return the best finite array found by FFT scoring.
        try:
            best_arr = scored[0][1] if scored else current_p
            final_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(best_arr)]
        except Exception:
            final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]

    if not final_list:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]

    return final_list
```
